# Endpoint matrix (generated)

Generated 2026-11-05 from `oryx-backend` controllers and `oryx-live-desktop` sources by the audit scripts. One row per backend action. **Current server check** is what the server enforces today; **Frontend call sites** are files that call the generated hook for the endpoint, and **Frontend keys** are the permission keys those files (and the permission-constant files they import) reference. **Proposed key** is the single key the endpoint should require: an existing key where one fits, otherwise a `NEW` draft name derived from the action, to be finalised in the domain sections of the main report.

Module/submodule is taken from the registration of the endpoint's current key, otherwise from the owning controller.

| Server enforcement today | Endpoints |
| --- | ---: |
| **sign-in only** | 895 |
| policy attribute | 449 |
| service check | 23 |
| controller check | 19 |
| **ANONYMOUS** | 15 |
| report filter (any-of) | 1 |
| **Total** | **1402** |
